- **deck-builder**: Build and quality-check PowerPoint decks on top of any corporate template. Reads the template and generates a brand pack, so the palette, grid, layouts, and logo zone are configuration rather than code. - **deck-copy**: Тексты презентаций по жанровым пресетам: сначала решается носитель (деку произносят, читают без спикера или и то и другое) — он задаёт бюджет слов на слайд, — дальше пресет из шести, скелет проходит titles-test, тело пишется по четырнадцати законам ядра и линтуется по правилам с id. Каждое измеримое утверждение сверено с первоисточником в паспорте цифр. Отличие от clarity: тот доводит до ясности любую русскую прозу, этот пишет текст деки по жанру. Вёрстка, графика и перевод — вне периметра. ([kyzdes/deck-copy-skill](https://github.com/kyzdes/deck-copy-skill))
- **deck-craft**: Self-contained slide-deck skill spanning three output targets from one body of deck craft: self-contained fixed-stage HTML presentations, lecturn.deck/1 JSON (and .pptx through the deckconv round-trip), and Diolog investor decks assembled from the 140-template / 12-recipe slide library. Carries its own visual-design layer rather than depending on a general design skill: consuming a supplied DESIGN.md or token file as binding context, authoring a direction when no brand exists (scheme / formality / density, subject-mined palettes, the signature element, the swap test), typography sized for pro… ([DiologIR/diolog-plugins](https://github.com/DiologIR/diolog-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/deck-craft) · 1 skill)

- **deck-master**: Builds editable .pptx decks from structured data with no presentation library, no template and no rendering engine. Diagram-first: architecture, flow and comparison slides rather than bullet lists. Text is measured against real Arial metrics before any box is sized, so nothing overflows; a geometric audit blocks collisions, off-canvas shapes and dangling connectors; output is validated against the published ISO-29500 schemas and is byte-for-byte deterministic. Runs on the Python standard library alone. ([mthanhlm/cgel-plugin](https://github.com/mthanhlm/cgel-plugin/tree/HEAD/deck-master) · 1 skill)
